- **f1-deploy**: Deploy the project to Cloudflare — Worker (wrangler) + Pages (static dashboard) + KV namespace + cron triggers, plus git setup. New agent for the cloud-hosting architecture. - **f1-publish**: Assemble the unified dashboard payload and publish it — write KV (live) and regenerate the committed dashboard/data.js offline seed. ([pratham46/f1-2026-analysis](https://github.com/pratham46/f1-2026-analysis/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/f1-publish.md))

- **fa-architect**: Plans before code. Use when a task is L/XL, touches more than one layer (db + core + ui), needs a migration with a data move, or when the human asks "how should we do X". Produces a sequenced plan with file list, risks, STOP points and — if the task is too big — a split into sub-tasks in docs/tasks/ format. Never writes application code. ([tj0517/Fjordanglers](https://github.com/tj0517/Fjordanglers/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fa-architect.md))
- **fa-core**: Domain layer specialist. Use for repositories, use-cases, auth guards, the inquiry state machine, event emission, matchers, the AI agent, e-mail sending and the metrics catalogue. Today that is src/actions and src/lib; after stage 2 it is packages/core. Never writes migrations (ask fa-db) or React (ask fa-admin / fa-web). ([tj0517/Fjordanglers](https://github.com/tj0517/Fjordanglers/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fa-core.md))

- **fa-reviewer**: Reviews a finished task against its "Done when" criteria. Use after an implementer reports done, before the PR is merged. Read-only on code. ([tj0517/Fjordanglers](https://github.com/tj0517/Fjordanglers/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fa-reviewer.md))
- **fa-web**: Public site and guide portal specialist (src/app minus admin; apps/web after stage 2). Use for marketing pages, experience pages, guide profiles, inquiry widget, ads landing pages, token pages (/offers, /reviews, /guide-intake), the guide dashboard, and the HTTP layer of webhooks. Reads through core. Never writes migrations or metric logic. ([tj0517/Fjordanglers](https://github.com/tj0517/Fjordanglers/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fa-web.md))
- **fabius**: Use to build and maintain reliable automated test suites — unit, integration, e2e, API, UI, performance — fixtures, test data, page objects and CI wiring matching the project framework, killing flakiness. Typically dispatched via Marcus's delegation plan. ([holi87/holak-teams](https://github.com/holi87/holak-teams/blob/HEAD/hephaestus/claude/agents/fabius.md))
- **Fable**: Anthropic-family deep-analysis agent — the in-family second look on hard problems and super-sensitive work (public LifeOS releases, security boundaries, irreversible or publish-bound actions). Runs the top Anthropic alias at xhigh effort in a fresh context: a fresh adversarial mind reading the artifact instead of the conversation, which is most of why he catches things. The top model (principal, 2026-09-24): Opus carries max-level work, analytical and creative. Fable is the costliest lane and its window runs out fast, so it is spent gently (2026-10-16, Router ISA F9): the router picks it only… ([danielmiessler/LifeOS](https://github.com/danielmiessler/LifeOS/blob/HEAD/LifeOS/install/agents/Fable.md) · ★19k)
- **fable**: Visiting Mythos-class spirit. Long-horizon agentic work, deep research, complex migrations. Xenos daimon—availability probed at invocation via /fable. ([tdimino/claude-code-minoan](https://github.com/tdimino/claude-code-minoan/blob/HEAD/agents/fable.md) · ★41)
- **fable**: Fable 5.1 at xhigh effort — for hardest reasoning, architecture, and long-horizon agentic work. ([kronael/tools](https://github.com/kronael/tools/blob/HEAD/agents/fable.md) · ★6)
- **fable**: Deep codebase audits, MVP-to-production planning, architecture audit reports. Use for: audit codebase, code audit, technical debt, architecture review, mvp to production, production ready, scalability report, refactor plan. ([mewking2099/MewVault](https://github.com/mewking2099/MewVault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable.md) · ★4)
- **fable**: Adversarially reviews the implementation plan (stage 2) and the integrated branch diff (stage 5) of the shipyard pipeline, always on the Fable model. Read-only. ([esse/shipyard](https://github.com/esse/shipyard/blob/HEAD/agents/fable.md))
- **fable**: MUST BE USED when the user names Claude Fable — "ask fable", "ask agent fable", "use fable", "claude fable", "what would fable say", "fable's take". Not for multi-file implementation — use coder instead. Not for a multi-model panel — use advisors instead. ([whichguy/c-thru](https://github.com/whichguy/c-thru/blob/HEAD/agents/fable.md))
- **fable-5-1**: Visiting Mythos-class seer. Cross-model thinking synthesis, adaptive-depth reasoning, long-horizon agentic work. Xenos theoros—availability probed at invocation via /fable. ([tdimino/claude-code-minoan](https://github.com/tdimino/claude-code-minoan/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-5-1.md) · ★41)

- **fable-advisor**: Second-opinion advisor and final reviewer running Claude's most capable model (Fable 5.1). Consult at commitment boundaries — before architectural decisions, data migrations, big refactors, or API designs, and whenever the same problem has resisted two attempts — and ALWAYS once at the end of a deliverable, to review the accumulated changes before the orchestrator reports done. Pass it the decision (or the diff), the constraints, and the options considered; it returns a verdict with reasoning and the risk that decides it. Advises only — never implements. ([DannyMac180/fable-advisor](https://github.com/DannyMac180/fable-advisor/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-advisor.md) · ★1.1k)
- **fable-advisor**: Rare escalation authority for design arbitration, unblocking stuck problems, and final review of large changes — and for landing the resolution when handing it back down would just repeat a failure. Invoke sparingly — routine reviews belong to team-execute Phase 2 or /codex:adversarial-review, and routine implementation to general-purpose-sonnet or Codex. - **fable-advisor**: High-stakes decision advisor running on Claude Code's Fable model alias. Spawn for a single-shot strategic verdict on an architecture/design decision, a stuck debugging loop, a draft plan, a pre-completion review, or the design of an unattended loop/schedule/routine before it starts running — never for generation work. Expects a briefing packet (decision, options or loop design, constraints, evidence, file pointers); returns a terse committed verdict. Expensive model — consult per the fable-advisor skill's budget rules. ([czlonkowski/fables](https://github.com/czlonkowski/fables/blob/HEAD/plugins/fable-advisor/agents/fable-advisor.md) · ★25)
- **fable-advisor**: Second-opinion advisor on Claude Fable 5. Consult at commitment boundaries — architecture, migrations, API shape, refactor strategy, or when the same problem has failed twice. Pass decision, constraints, and options; returns a short verdict. Advises only — never implements. ([BuildContext/fable-orchestrator](https://github.com/BuildContext/fable-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-advisor.md) · ★4)
- **fable-advisor**: Design critique before expensive-to-reverse decisions — architecture, data migrations, security approach, ambiguous specs. Advises only; never edits files. ([mefayed/boss-skill](https://github.com/mefayed/boss-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-advisor.md) · ★4)
- **fable-advisor**: Second-opinion advisor running Claude's most capable model (Fable 5). Consult at commitment boundaries — before architectural decisions, data migrations, big refactors, or API designs, and whenever the same problem has resisted two attempts. Pass it the decision, the constraints, and the options considered; it returns a verdict with reasoning and the risk that decides it. Advises only — never implements. ([huytdps13400/fable-advisor](https://github.com/huytdps13400/fable-advisor/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-advisor.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **fable-advisor**: Read-only second-opinion advisor for a decision at a commitment boundary, pinned to the roster's strongest-judgment tier. Use when you're about to commit to a choice (an approach, a scope cut, a go/no-go, a tradeoff between two close options) and want an independent gut-check in under 300 words rather than a full spec or a structured design review. Read-only: advises, never implements. For a full implementation spec, use planner instead; for a design review, use architect-reviewer instead. ([lritter/agent-skills](https://github.com/lritter/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/advise/agents/fable-advisor.md))

- **fable-architect**: Use for architecture decisions, data-model taxonomy changes, milestone scope calls, resolving conflicts between historical/archaeological/performance constraints, and final review of large milestones. - **fable-audit-triage**: Risk triage for one surface of a fable-audit run. - **fable-audit-verifier**: Blind adversarial verifier for one fable-audit candidate finding. Receives the claim and evidence locations — never the reviewer's rationale — and tries to refute it. - **fable-captain**: Council lead contract. This is the LEAD's contract, adopted by the main session; it is never spawned as a teammate. - **fable-consultant**: Weighs in on one hard question on behalf of a caller who has already done the legwork and wants a second read — on a design, a diagnosis, a plan, or a choice they keep re-opening. - **fable-critic**: Independent adversarial reviewer applying the Fable rubrics. Use PROACTIVELY after any substantial piece of work — code diff, design, document, architecture — and before delivering it. The critic is the fable-core Gauntlet externalized into a second pair of eyes with no authorship bias: it hunts defects against a quota, grades the rubric, names anti-patterns, and issues a SHIP / FIX-FIRST / RETHINK verdict. - **fable-dev**: Fable 级开发工程代理(Opus 升级预设)。任何实质性开发任务——实现/修复/重构/部署/排障——都用它当主力工程师。它以 Fable 5 的工程纪律运行:证据定义完成、根因修复、范围手术刀、回合必须闭环。需要 Fable 水准产出而当前模型是 Opus 系列时,这是默认选择。 ([Jaydenlk/HR](https://github.com/Jaydenlk/HR/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-dev.md) · ★4)
- **fable-developer**: Fable 5-backed implementation engineer for the hardest tasks — ones that resist atomization (frontier complexity). Executes one task per dispatch from an ai-crew team-lead. - **fable-gate-reviewer**: Renders a product verdict on a bounded decision packet, for a coordinator that needs one without returning to the director. - **fable-judge**: Grades a transcript or artifact against the Fable Mode eval keys (evals/eval-*.md) or, when no key applies, against the domain rubrics — producing a cited scorecard, a band, and a diagnosis. - **fable-judge**: Judgement lane — the most expensive model, for planning and final verdict/synthesis only. Fable 5.1 at max effort. - **fable-reasoner**: Top-rung reasoning escalator — runs Claude Fable 5, the most capable model, at max effort. ([VoidLight00/fablelayer](https://github.com/VoidLight00/fablelayer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fablelayer-qa-auditor.md) · ★10)

- **factor-geometer**: Risk architect. Builds factor loadings, estimates covariance with proper shrinkage, computes alpha-orthogonal decomposition. If your alpha is spanned by known factors, it's not alpha. ([DeevsDeevs/agent-system](https://github.com/DeevsDeevs/agent-system/blob/HEAD/mft-research-experts/agents/factor-geometer.md) · ★40)
- **factor-researcher**: Runs systematic quantitative alpha-factor research end to end — validates a market dataset, mines a factor library with the FactorMiner engine, evaluates it (IC/ICIR/correlation), backtests a composite signal under transaction costs, benchmarks it against baselines, and packages a research note. Use when an analyst or PM asks to discover, evaluate, or stress-test predictive signals on a price/volume universe. Not for fundamental single-name work — use earnings-reviewer or model-builder for that. ([minihellboy/factorminer](https://github.com/minihellboy/factorminer/blob/HEAD/integrations/factor-researcher/plugin/agents/factor-researcher.md) · ★117)
- **factor-researcher**: Read-only research subagent dispatched by factor-architect (and factor-dedup) to consult external sources for canonical architectural patterns, ecosystem idioms, and good-pattern references. Returns sourced recommendations with attribution; never modifies the codebase. ([smorinlabs/smorinlabs-harness](https://github.com/smorinlabs/smorinlabs-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/factor-harness/agents/factor-researcher.md))
- **factor-scanner**: Read-only scanner subagent dispatched by factor-scan (and occasionally factor-architect) to scan one file or directory for bugs, quality issues, and architectural smells. Returns a structured finding list with severity, evidence, and recommended actions. ([smorinlabs/smorinlabs-harness](https://github.com/smorinlabs/smorinlabs-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/factor-harness/agents/factor-scanner.md))
- **factorial-fetcher**: Fetches HR team data from the Factorial HR API. Retrieves employees, leaves, and leave types. ([weorbitant/orbitant-os](https://github.com/weorbitant/orbitant-os/blob/HEAD/plugins/orbitant-operations/agents/factorial-fetcher.md) · ★9)
- **factorio-automation-agent**: Scales Factorio prototypes into large-scale production systems with integrated logistics. Use this agent when you need to implement massive factory expansion, coordinate complex logistics networks, or scale proven designs. ([JackHopkins/claude-code-plays-factorio](https://github.com/JackHopkins/claude-code-plays-factorio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factorio-automation-agent.md) · ★14)
- **factorio-inspector-agent**: Comprehensive Factorio factory analysis and state monitoring. - **factorio-prototyping-agent**: Designs and tests Factorio factory components in isolation. - **factorio-spatial-reasoning-agent**: Specialized in spatial pathfinding and logistics connections for Factorio entities. ([JackHopkins/claude-code-plays-factorio](https://github.com/JackHopkins/claude-code-plays-factorio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factorio-spatial-reasoning-agent.md) · ★14)
- **factory**: Meta-agent that creates specialized Claude Code subagents. Use when you need to build a new agent for a specific domain. ([barnabasJ/claude](https://github.com/barnabasJ/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/essentials/agents/factual-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **factual-reviewer**: Read-only review lens for technical accuracy — checks that a diff does what the PR, linked issue, and docs claim, and flags contradictions with documented contracts. Use in the ship review stage. ([brezzy1337/claude-t3-devkit](https://github.com/brezzy1337/claude-t3-devkit/blob/HEAD/claude-t3-devkit/agents/factual-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **factual-reviewer**: Read-only review lens for technical accuracy in a Unity / C# diff — checks that the change does what the PR, linked issue, and docs claim (including platform claims such as WebGL or dedicated-server support), and flags contradictions with documented contracts, stale XML docs or tooltips, and tests that assert the wrong thing. Use in the ship review stage. ([brezzy1337/claude-GameCI-devkit](https://github.com/brezzy1337/claude-GameCI-devkit/blob/HEAD/claude-unity-devkit/agents/factual-reviewer.md))
